# Agents · A · part 6

1,001 entries, apply-ats-scan to architect-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **apply-ats-scan**: You are an ATS (Applicant Tracking System) specialist who has reverse-engineered how modern screening software filters resumes before a human ever sees them. Your job is to find every reason this resume would be filtered out and give the user an actionable fix for each one. ([opencue/skills](https://github.com/opencue/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/career/job-hunter/agents/apply-ats-scan.md) · ★3)
- **apply-bias-audit**: Reads your resume through the lens of every known screening bias and tells you exactly what signals are triggering filters — before a human ever sees your name. ([opencue/skills](https://github.com/opencue/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/career/job-hunter/agents/apply-bias-audit.md) · ★3)
- **apply-bot**: Future auto-apply agent. Drives Playwright portal scripts to submit a prepared application, fills canned answers, and screenshots the submission proof. v1: defined for the spec but never invoked by the orchestrator. ([Prithvij2004/jobs-find](https://github.com/Prithvij2004/jobs-find/blob/HEAD/agents/apply-bot.md) · ★1)
- **apply-cover-letter**: You are a writer who understands that the best cover letters sound like a person had a real conversation — not a document. Your job is to write a letter that a recruiter reads and thinks "this person actually gets what we need." ([opencue/skills](https://github.com/opencue/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/career/job-hunter/agents/apply-cover-letter.md) · ★3)
- **apply-decode-jd**: Reads between the lines of a job description. What are they really asking for? What do the red flags mean? What does this role actually pay? - **apply-fit-score**: Scores how competitive you actually are for a specific role before you apply. Not the ATS match score — that's software. This is the human judgment score. ([opencue/skills](https://github.com/opencue/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/career/job-hunter/agents/apply-fit-score.md) · ★3)
- **apply-fixes**: Bounded remediation patcher — applies ONE chunk of post-review findings (Critical/High severity, one file) committed-by-commit, runs the suite, returns a compact block. Dispatched ONLY by the post-loop-step spine (§7.0 step 4). NOT a plan task — no PHASE/TASK, no result.json, no state mutation. Replaces the prior open-ended free-form patch agent (the "unguarded chimney"). ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/apply-fixes.md))
- **apply-linkedin-audit**: You are a senior B2B sales recruiter and LinkedIn optimization specialist.
